# Signal history: each method in each 15-minute slot

The detail behind [signal-history-by-15m-slot.md](signal-history-by-15m-slot.md): for every method, every 15-minute slot of the session (5:35 PM to 5:29 PM IST) in which it closed a trade. 5.7 days of history, 30 Sep 2026, 6:59 PM to 6 Oct 2026, 11:15 AM.

> **Most rows here are too thin to mean anything.** A method-and-slot pair has six trades on average, from under six days, often several from one price move. Rows with fewer than 10 closed trades are marked **thin**. Use this to look something up, not to pick a slot.

Columns are the same as in the main report: closed trades, target hits, stop hits, time-outs, win rate (closed in profit ÷ closed) and net BTC points before fees.
